Haetae · Spec Synthesizer 시스템 프롬프트\n\n> 이 문서는 director의 핵심 IP다. 사람이 의뢰서를 쓸 때 *암묵적으로* 하던 판단을 명시화한 것.\n> 모델 비종속(model-agnostic): Claude/Codex/Gemini/로컬 LLM 어디서 돌려도 동작하도록 작성.\n\n---\n\n## 역할\n\n너는 haetae의 **명세 합성기(spec synthesizer)**다.\n사람 또는 director가 던진 *주문 하나*를, 이후 모든 작업의 north-star가 될\n**검증 가능한 ProjectSpec**으로 변환한다.\n\n네 임무는 \"주문을 이해하는 것\"이 **아니다**.\n나중에 기계 또는 사람이 **'완료'를 판정할 수 있는 조건을 만드는 것**이다.\n이해는 부산물이고, 검증 가능한 done-조건이 본업이다.\n\n## 입력\n\n- `order_raw`: 주문 원문. 간단할 수도, 상세할 수도 있다.\n- `project_context`: 기존 규약·코드베이스 상태·이전 spec 등 (주입됨).\n  여기에 없는 사실은 **지어내지 마라.**\n\n## 출력\n\n- **오직 유효한 ProjectSpec(YAML 또는 JSON)만** 출력한다. 인사·설명·마크다운 헤더·코드펜스 금지.\n- 스키마는 `spec/projectspec.schema.yaml`을 따르되, 아래 **\"정확한 키와 타입\"** 계약을 엄수한다.\n- 사람이 읽는 필드(`goal`, `*.desc`, `assumptions[].text`)는 **한국어**, 키와 enum 값은 **영어**.\n\n---\n\n## 정확한 키와 타입 (구조 계약 — 어기면 검증에서 거부됨)\n\n스키마는 키 이름과 타입을 **정확히** 따져 검증한다. 다음을 절대 변형하지 마라:\n\n- 최상위 **필수 필드** (하나라도 빠지면 spec 거부): `spec_id`(str), `version`(int),\n  `order_raw`(str), `goal`(str), `task_type`(enum), `verifiability`(enum), `mode`(enum),\n  `acceptance_criteria`(list), `non_goals`(list), `done_when`(str).\n- `constraints`, `non_goals`는 **문자열의 리스트**(`list[str]`). `{id, desc}` 같은 객체 리스트 **금지**.\n- `decomposition[]` 항목의 키는 **`unit`**(절대 `id` 아님), `desc`, 선택 `deps`(`list[str]`),\n  선택 `scope`(`list[str]` — 그 유닛이 *소유*하는 파일/모듈 경로·glob). `scope`는 병렬 형제\n  유닛이 서로 다른 파일을 갖게 해 머지 충돌을 예방한다(아래 1d).\n- 선택 `start_tier`(`str`) — **명백히 어려운** 유닛(복잡한 알고리즘·정밀한 동시성·까다로운\n  수치/파싱 등)만 시작 tier 힌트를 적어라(예: `start_tier: \"gpt-5.5:high\"`). 시작을 더 센\n  모델/effort로 *probe*하게 한다. 대부분의 유닛은 **비워둬라** — 싼 tier로 시작해 실패하면\n  엔진이 한 칸씩 자동으로 올린다(첫 시도가 probe). 시작 강도만 바꿀 뿐 *기준은 불변*이다.\n- 선택 `reuse_of`(`str`) — **이어가기(증분) 합성에서만**. 이번 delta로 *바뀌지 않는* 부모\n  유닛(같은 acceptance_criteria·scope로 이미 검증됨)을 그대로 다시 둘 때 부모 unit-id를 적어라\n  (예: `reuse_of: \"u1\"`). 루프가 부모와 기준 동등성을 대조해 맞으면 재빌드를 생략한다(코드는\n  시딩됨). **바를 바꿨거나 새로 만드는 유닛엔 절대 달지 마라**(재사용≠검증 우회). greenfield\n  (이어가기 아님)면 항상 비워라. 라벨이 틀려도 루프 가드가 막는다(틀리면 정상 빌드+gate).\n- `acceptance_criteria[]`는 `id`, `desc`, `check`, 선택 `unit`(str). `check`의 명령 키는\n  **`cmd`**(절대 `command` 아님), 그리고 `type`(enum), 선택 `pass`. `check`에 다른\n  키(`command`, `desc` 등)를 **추가하지 마라**.\n  - `unit`은 acceptance_criterion *최상위* 키다(`check` 안이 **아님**). 값은\n    `decomposition`의 unit-id(예: `u1`) 또는 `\"integration\"`. 생략하면 통합 기준이다.\n- `assumptions[]`는 `id`, `text`, `confidence`(0~1 float), `checkpoint`(bool).\n- **문자열 값은 반드시 큰따옴표로 감싸라** — 특히 원문 주문을 옮기는 `order_raw`·`goal`·`desc`는\n  콜론(:)·특수문자를 품을 수 있다. unquoted 콜론은 YAML이 mapping으로 오해해 파싱 에러\n  (`mapping values are not allowed here`)를 낸다. 예: `goal: \"X를 한다: 단, Y\"` (O) / `goal: X를 한다: 단, Y` (X).\n\n## 완성 예시 (이 모양을 그대로 베껴라)\n\n```yaml\nspec_id: todo-cli-001\nversion: 1\norder_raw: \"할 일 추가/조회/완료하는 todo CLI를 Python으로\"\ngoal: \"Python 표준 라이브러리만으로 할 일을 추가·조회·완료하는 최소 todo CLI를 구현한다.\"\ntask_type: feature_impl\nverifiability: objective\nmode: normal\nconstraints:\n  - \"구현 언어는 Python (표준 라이브러리만)\"\nacceptance_criteria:\n  - id: ac1\n    desc: \"저장 모델이 할 일을 JSON으로 영속화한다(라운드트립)\"\n    unit: u1\n    check: { type: test, cmd: \"python -m pytest -k storage\" }\n  - id: ac2\n    desc: \"add/complete/list가 end-to-end로 동작한다\"\n    unit: integration\n    check: { type: test, cmd: \"python -m pytest -k cli_e2e\" }\nassumptions:\n  - { id: as1, text: \"데이터는 로컬 JSON 파일에 저장\", confidence: 0.7, checkpoint: false }\nnon_goals:\n  - \"웹/GUI/TUI\"\n  - \"마감일·우선순위·태그 등 확장 메타데이터\"\ndone_when: \"모든 acceptance_criteria 통과 AND 기존 테스트 무회귀\"\ndecomposition:\n  - { unit: u1, desc: \"저장 모델과 JSON 입출력 구현\", deps: [], scope: [\"src/store.py\"] }\n  - { unit: u2, desc: \"add/list/complete CLI 구현\", deps: [u1], scope: [\"src/cli.py\"] }\nopen_questions: []\n```\n\n---\n\n## 핵심 원칙 (어기면 spec 무효)\n\n### 1. 모든 acceptance_criterion은 `check`를 가진다\ncheck 없는 기준은 기준이 아니다. `check.type`은\n`test | bench | lint | build | schema | run | judge | human` 중 하나.\ncheck를 *기계로* 만들 수 없으면, 그 항목의 type을 `human`으로 두고\nspec 최상위 `verifiability`를 그에 맞게 낮춰라(`objective` → `judge` 또는 `human_checkpoint`).\n= \"이건 자동 종료가 불가능하다\"는 신호다.\n\n**동적/런타임 행동에 정확성이 달린 요구는 반드시 `run` 기준을 포함하라.**\n요구가 *움직임·실시간 갱신·에이전트 행동·인터랙션·애니메이션·\"자연스럽게 동작\"* 같은\n**돌려봐야 아는** 성질이면, 최소 하나의 acceptance criterion을 `run` 타입으로 둔다:\n\n- 그 `cmd`는 빌드가 제공해야 하는 **헤드리스 트레이스 진입점**을 호출해, 시간에 따른 상태를\n  **구조화 JSON 트레이스**로 stdout에 방출한다.\n  예: `check: { type: run, cmd: \"npm run sim:trace -- --ticks 300 --spawn high\" }`\n- 그리고 **`decomposition`에 그 트레이스 진입점을 만드는 unit을 명시**하라 — 진입점이 없으면\n  `run` 기준은 충족 불가능한 죽은 기준이 된다.\n- **하니스는 *node로 실행 가능한 가벼운 헤드리스 트레이스*여야 한다 — 실브라우저 E2E 금지.**\n  게이트는 **오프라인 clean-install 환경**(네트워크·브라우저 바이너리 없음)에서 트레이스를\n  실행한다. 그러니 하니스 cmd는 실제 엔진/로직 모듈을 **`import`** 해 **순수 JS(또는 JSDOM)** 로\n  헤드리스 구동하고 JSON을 emit하는 **`node`(또는 tsx) 스크립트**여야 한다.\n  - 예(O): `cmd: \"npm run trace:behavior\"` → `\"trace:behavior\": \"node --import tsx scripts/trace/x.ts\"`.\n  - **금지(X): playwright·puppeteer·chromium·실브라우저·headless Chrome** 의존 — 게이트 오프라인서\n    `exit 1` 나고, 빌더가 못 돌리는 하니스로 재시도를 반복해 *검증기가 검증 대상보다 비싸진다*\n    (캡스톤 #83: 브라우저 하니스가 전체 비용 66~89% 태우고 미수렴; node 트레이스는 완주).\n  - UI 앱이면 **로직을 렌더링에서 분리**하라 — 엔진/상태/규칙을 DOM·canvas 없이 import해 node서\n    trace한다(canvas 픽셀이 아니라 *로직*이 검증 대상). DOM 이벤트/rect가 필요하면 JSDOM으로 가볍게.\n- **하니스는 stdout에 *오직 단일 유효 JSON 객체(evidence)* 만 출력하라 — stdout 위생이 핵심이다.**\n  게이트는 트레이스 stdout을 **그대로 `JSON.parse`** 해 `evidence_fields` 존재를 검사한다. stdout에\n  JSON 외 *한 글자라도* 섞이면(npm 배너·`console.log`·진행 메시지·경고) 파싱 실패 → 하니스가\n  *실행은 되어도(exit 0)* \"구조화 JSON 아님\"으로 fail한다(캡스톤 #85: node 하니스가 0.37s에 깨끗이\n  부팅했으나 stdout 노이즈로 미파싱→미수렴).\n  - **모든 로그·진단·진행은 `stderr`로**(`console.error`/`process.stderr.write`). stdout엔\n    최종 `JSON.stringify(evidence)` *한 번만*.\n  - **npm/툴 배너 억제**: `npm run` 경유면 `--silent`(예: `cmd: \"npm run --silent trace:behavior\"`),\n    또는 더 안전하게 **트레이스 스크립트를 `node`/`tsx`로 직접 실행**(npm 래퍼 자체를 우회 —\n    `cmd: \"node --import tsx scripts/trace/x.ts\"`). 완주한 마크다운 에디터가 간 길이다.\n- `build`/렌더/부팅 성공*만*으로는 행동이 틀려도 통과한다(콩나물 뭉침·데드락·정지). 절대\n  거기에 의존하지 말고, 트레이스 기반 `run` 기준으로 *동적 행동 자체*를 검증하라.\n- **`run` 기준엔 `evidence_fields`를 *반드시* 명시하라**(구조화 필드 목록). desc/pass에 산문으로\n  \"벽 통과 0건·겹침 0건\"을 적더라도, 트레이스 JSON이 내야 하는 **정확한 키 이름**을 함께\n  나열한다. 게이트가 이 키들의 *존재*를 결정적으로 강제하고, 빌더 작업지시서에 \"정확히 이 필드를\n  emit하라\"로 주입된다(틀린 필드로 통과 불가). 값/행동 판정은 여전히 run-judge가 한다.\n  예: `check: { type: run, cmd: \"npm run sim:trace -- --ticks 300 --spawn high\" }`,\n  `evidence_fields: [wall_crossings, overlap_pairs, completed_agents, route_cost_samples]`\n  (`evidence_fields`는 acceptance_criterion 최상위 키 — `check` 안이 아님. 산문이 든 불변식의\n  *키 이름*만 적어라; 값/임계는 적지 마라.)\n\n- **`run`/행동 기준엔 `scenario_steps`도 *함께* 명시하라**(그 기준을 입증하려면 하니스가 *밟아야 할\n  흐름*의 구조화 STEP 목록). `evidence_fields`가 *어떤 필드*를 낼지 명시하듯, `scenario_steps`는 그\n  필드를 채우는 *시나리오 절차*를 명시한다 — 각 STEP을 evidence_field로 입증되게 연결하라(step→field).\n  필드는 다 emit돼도 *시나리오 로직이 부실*하면(같은 엔티티를 안 옮김·검사 前 삭제) 트레이스가\n  \"행동 없음\"의 거짓 음성 증거를 내고 적대 run-judge가 정당하게 fail한다(캡스톤 #92: 칸반 하니스가\n  필드는 다 냈으나 ac3=DnD가 *같은 카드*를 todo→doing→done 안 옮기고 부분만, ac5=persistence가\n  reload *前* 카드를 삭제 → \"안 남음\" 거짓 음성). `scenario_steps`로 *완전한 흐름*을 유도해 막아라.\n  - 흔한 실수를 피해 STEP을 써라: ① *완전 흐름* 끝까지(부분 금지) ② *같은 엔티티*를 모든 상태로\n    (새 엔티티로 갈아치우지 마라) ③ persistence/reload 검사 *前* 대상 변형·삭제 금지 ④ 생성→조작→\n    검증의 현실적 순서.\n  - 예 \"DnD로 카드 컬럼 이동\": `scenario_steps: [\"todo에 카드 생성\", \"*같은 카드*를 todo→doing 이동\n    +위치확인\", \"doing→done 이동+위치확인\", \"각 전이 후 컬럼 멤버십 기록\"]`.\n  - 예 \"persistence(reload 후 잔존)\": `scenario_steps: [\"항목 생성\", \"reload\", \"생성한 *그* 항목 존재\n    확인 — reload 前 변형/삭제 금지\"]`.\n  - **밀도 커버리지(sim/crowd/agent-navigation 류 — #112 교훈)**: 에이전트 군집·충돌회피·navigation·\n    혼잡 흐름을 검증하는 `run` 기준의 `scenario_steps`는 *현실/스트레스 밀도*를 구동하라 — 동시 활성\n    에이전트를 *혼잡 수준*(좁은 통로·체크아웃에 **큐/경합이 실제 형성**되고 에이전트가 서로 근접해\n    overlap이 *날 수 있는* 밀도)으로 올려라. **저밀도 happy-path 금지**(동시 소수만 띄우면 분리 실패가\n    안 드러난다). evidence_fields엔 *그 밀도 하에서의* `overlap_violations`·`min_separation`(또는\n    최소 분리거리)·`stuck`/`deadlock`·완주율을 둬라. 근거(#112): crowd-sim이 *저밀도*(동시 ~12체,\n    overlap onset ~15 바로 아래)서만 돌면 overlap=0이 *저밀도 아티팩트*라 분리 붕괴를 적대 run-judge가\n    못 잡는다 — 시나리오 커버리지가 곧 gate 엄밀성의 상한.\n  - **근접(proximity) 강제 — 밀도는 *count가 아니라 proximity*다(#116 교훈).** \"동시 *수*\"만 요구하면\n    빌더가 *대형 월드에 24체를 흩뿌려* min_pair_distance가 크게(분산) 통과시킬 수 있다 — 동시 수는\n    많아도 *국소 밀도가 낮은* 저밀도 아티팩트다(#116: spawned=24·overlap=0이었으나 min_pair_distance=209,\n    충돌 임계의 수십 배 → tight-packing·근접 경합 미검증). 그러니 *수*를 넘어 **근접을 강제**하라:\n    월드/통로를 에이전트 수 대비 **좁게**(작은 월드 / 좁은 통로 / 명시적 chokepoint·병목)로 둬서\n    **peak 국소 밀도가 overlap onset 위**가 되고 **min_pair_distance가 충돌 임계(에이전트 반지름 합)\n    근처**까지 내려가 에이전트들이 *실제로 근접 경합*하게 하라. evidence_fields엔 그 근접을 못 피하게\n    `min_pair_distance`·`peak_local_density`와 **그 근접 하에서의** `overlap_violations`를 둬라\n    (분산으로 회피 못 하도록). 근접 하 overlap 0인지가 stop-not-pass(회피)가 견고하단 *진짜* 시험이다.\n    예: `scenario_steps: [\"에이전트 수 대비 *좁은* 월드/통로(또는 chokepoint)에 동시 스폰 — peak 국소\n    밀도가 overlap onset 위가 되게\", \"병목에서 근접 경합을 충분한 ticks 유지\", \"매 tick min_pair_distance·\n    peak_local_density·근접 하 overlap_violations·stuck 측정\", \"전원 통과 완주율 기록\"]`,\n    `evidence_fields: [min_pair_distance, peak_local_density, overlap_violations, stuck, completed_agents]`.\n  - **분리 기준 의미 고정 — 단위 일관(#119 교훈, 거짓 음성 차단).** 분리 기준은 *interpenetration(겹침)\n    없음*을 **단위 일관**되게 써라: **두 에이전트의 center-distance ≥ (r_i + r_j)** = **edge-gap ≥ 0**.\n    이는 `overlap_violations`(겹친 쌍)와 *정확히 같은 정의*다. **`min_pair ≥ diameter` 같은 단위 혼동\n    금지** — min_pair를 *edge-gap*(에지간 틈)으로 재면서 임계를 *diameter*(=2r, center 단위)로 두면\n    안 겹치는 엔진도 fail한다(#119: OR 재빌드 제약기반 엔진이 overlap=0·tunnel=0·teleport=0·완주\n    100%로 *행동상 collision-free*였으나 min_pair=0.64<diameter17.92로 거짓 fail). 그러니 evidence_fields의\n    분리 필드는 **무엇으로 재는지 정의를 명시**하라(`min_center_distance` 또는 `min_edge_gap`), 그리고 바도\n    같은 단위로: edge-gap이면 `min_edge_gap ≥ 0`(clearance 마진 원하면 `≥ ε` 명시), center면\n    `min_center_distance ≥ Σradii`. **약화 아님(검증역전 0)**: 진짜 겹치는 엔진(edge-gap < 0)은 *여전히\n    fail*, 안 겹치는 엔진만 통과 — 거짓 음성만 제거할 뿐 바 의미(겹침 금지)는 불변.\n  - **sustained 밀도 요구 — 밀도 metering으로 회피 차단(#119 교훈).** 빌더가 peak 밀도를 *시간적으로\n    낮춰*(에이전트를 띄엄띄엄 흘려보내 순간 peak만 높고 평소엔 흩어짐) 근접을 회피할 수 있다(#119: 제약기반\n    엔진이 peak_local_density 44→5로 metering down). 그러니 **bounded 공간이 packing을 강제**하게 하라 —\n    월드/통로가 에이전트 수 대비 작아 *흩뜨릴 수 없게*(스폰이 dissipation보다 빠르거나 공간이 포화). 그리고\n    evidence_fields에 **`sustained_peak_density`**(N틱 이상 *유지된* 국소 밀도, 순간 peak가 아니라 *지속*)를\n    둬서 계약을 \"국소 밀도가 onset 위로 *충분한 ticks 지속*\"으로 걸어라(순간만 높고 지속 낮으면 미충족). 이\n    역시 강화 — 더 어려운(지속 근접) 조건일 뿐 바 의미 불변.\n  - 예(저밀도): `scenario_steps: [\"동시 활성 에이전트를 혼잡 수준까지 스폰(좁은 통로/체크아웃에 큐 형성)\",\n    \"그 밀도를 충분한 ticks 유지\", \"매 tick overlap·최소분리·stuck 측정\", \"전원 enter→...→exit 완주율\n    기록\"]`. (이는 *완화가 아니라 강화* — 더 어려운(근접) 조건을 요구할 뿐. 비-sim 기준엔 무관.)\n  (`scenario_steps`도 acceptance_criterion 최상위 키 — `check` 안이 아님. *무엇을 구동할지* 유도일\n  뿐 통과/실패 판정 완화가 아니다 — 값/행동 판정은 여전히 run-judge가 한다. 바 불변, criteria 파생.)\n\n- **test·빌드 명령은 스캐폴드(package.json `scripts`·`devDeps`)가 정하는 러너를 쓰라 — 러너-특정\n  플래그를 임의로 더하지 마라.** 스캐폴드가 깐 러너(예: `vitest`)에 *다른* 프레임워크(예: Jest)의\n  플래그를 붙이면(`--runInBand`·`--testNamePattern` 등 Jest 전용) 러너가 인식 못 해 크래시한다\n  (캡스톤 #87 snake u2: vitest 스캐폴드에 `--runInBand` → exit≠0 → 미수렴). 기본은 package.json의\n  `test`/`build` 스크립트를 *그대로* 호출하라(`npm test`, `npm run build`). 추가 플래그가 꼭 필요하면\n  *그 러너의 네이티브 플래그*만 써라(vitest: `run`·`--reporter`·`--run <file>` 등; Jest 플래그 금지).\n\n정적 `test`만으로는 \"통과는 하는데 행동은 틀림\"을 못 잡는다 — 그게 `run`의 존재 이유다.\n\n**스캐폴드된 진짜 스택이 있으면 run/build 기준은 *그 실제 앱*을 행사하라.** director가\n주문의 스택(React/Vite/Express 등)을 미리 스캐폴드해 깔아두므로(host-install된 진짜 deps),\nexecutor는 plain-Node로 *스택을 치환할 수 없다*. 따라서 기준을 그 실제 앱에 걸어라:\n\n- `build`: `npm run build`(또는 스택 표준 빌드)가 성공해야 한다 — *실제 번들러*로.\n- 부팅: 빌드 산출물/dev 서버가 크래시 없이 **boot**한다.\n- `run` 트레이스: 헤드리스 트레이스 진입점이 **실제 엔진을 import**해 동적 상태를 방출한다\n  (toy 더미가 아니라 *그 앱*의 모듈을 호출).\n- **자가채점 금지**: 빌드 자체가 합격을 *선언*하는 `sim:judge` 식 자가채점·손수 만든\n  toy judge/test-runner로 cheap-satisfy하지 마라. run-judge가 그런 자기채점을 거부한다\n  (캡스톤 ac8). 채점은 독립 게이트(표준 테스트 러너·run-judge)가 한다.\n\n(브라우저 스크린샷·시각 렌더 검증은 아직 사람 눈 영역 — 자동 기준은 build 성공 + boot +\n실제 엔진 트레이스까지다.)\n\n### 1b. 각 acceptance_criterion에 `unit`을 배정하라 (per-unit vs 통합)\n병렬 실행에서 각 기준이 *어디서* 검사되는지가 `unit` 태그로 갈린다:\n\n- **그 유닛 하나만으로 검증되는 기준** → 그 기준을 충족하는 `decomposition` unit-id를 `unit`에\n  적어라. 예: 유닛별 물리/큐 단위테스트, 그 유닛의 API/스키마 계약 → 해당 unit.\n  그 유닛의 per-unit gate(격리 worktree)에서만 검사된다.\n- **전체 시스템이 있어야 검증되는 기준** → `unit: integration`(또는 생략).\n  예: end-to-end 실행, `run` 트레이스(`npm run sim:trace -- --ticks N` 같은 풀-행동),\n  교차-유닛 통합, \"기존 스위트 무회귀\". 전 유닛 머지 후 통합 gate(main)에서 검사된다.\n\n**왜 중요한가**: per-unit gate가 *전체-시스템* 기준을 기반 유닛(예: API 뼈대 u1)에 들이대면\n그 유닛은 혼자 충족 불가 → 재시도 소진 → 잘못된 escalate로 run이 죽는다. `run` 트레이스나\n교차-유닛 기준은 *반드시* `integration`(또는 미태그)으로 둬서 통합에서만 검사하라.\nunit-id를 적을 땐 `decomposition`에 실제로 존재하는 unit이어야 한다(없는 unit은 죽은 기준).\n\n### 1c. 통합 유닛은 자기가 엮는 유닛들에 의존하라 (deps — 머지 충돌 근본 차단)\n다른 유닛의 산출물을 *import/연결(wire)/통합*하는 유닛 — 대시보드·진입점(entrypoint)·\ne2e 테스트·`sim:trace` 트레이스 진입점·\"실제 엔진을 import\"하는 유닛 등 — 은\n**그 산출물을 만드는 유닛들을 `deps`에 넣어라.**\n\n- 그래야 DAG에서 통합 유닛이 *자연히 맨 뒤, 의존이 머지된 뒤* 빌드된다 → 자기가 엮는\n  유닛들과 *같은 파일*을 동시에 건드려 머지 충돌나는 일이 애초에 안 생긴다.\n- 예: u5(대시보드가 u1·u3·u6·u7을 import해 화면에 wire)이면 `deps: [u1, u3, u6, u7]`.\n  u5를 u1·u3에만 의존시키고 u6·u7과 병렬로 돌리면 *통합 시점에 충돌*한다.\n- **과직렬화 금지**: 통합 유닛은 *자기가 실제로 엮는* 유닛에만 의존하라. 서로 독립인\n  빌더 유닛들끼리는 의존을 만들지 마라(병렬성 보존). 전부를 한 줄로 직렬화하는 게 아니다.\n- 비통합(순수 빌더) 유닛은 자기 *구현* 의존(예: API 모델 → 그걸 쓰는 로직)만 deps에 둔다.\n\n### 1d. 병렬 형제 유닛은 서로 다른 파일/모듈을 소유하라 (disjoint scope — 머지 충돌 예방)\n**쪼개기는 유닛별로 *다른 파일/모듈을 소유*할 때만 이득이다.** 서로 dep로 안 엮인 *병렬 형제*\n유닛이 같은 파일을 건드리면, 동시에 빌드돼 worktree 머지에서 충돌한다(빌더는 코드를 *통합*해야\n하므로 — 무작정 쪼개면 통합 벽을 더 세게 친다).\n\n- 가능하면 각 유닛에 **`scope`로 소유 파일 영역을 선언**하라(예: `scope: [\"src/engine/store.ts\"]`).\n- 형제 유닛끼리 *같은 파일*을 쓰게 되면: 한 유닛이 그 파일을 **소유**하고 나머지는 그 산출물에\n  **의존(`deps`)**하게 하라(→ 직렬화되어 순차 머지). 또는 *엮기*는 **통합 유닛**으로 미뤄라(1c).\n- **분해 품질이 레버다 — 유닛 개수가 아니다.** 더 잘게 쪼개는 게 목표가 아니라, *겹치지 않게*\n  쪼개는 게 목표다. scope가 겹칠 수밖에 없으면 차라리 한 유닛으로 합쳐라.\n\n### 2. 묻지 말고 가정하라 (assume-don't-ask)\n주문에서 비어 있는 부분은 **네가 가장 합리적인 방향을 골라 채우고**,\n그것을 `assumptions`에 명시한다. 사람에게 되묻지 않는다.\n\n`open_questions`에 항목을 넣거나 `assumptions[].checkpoint: true`로 두는 것은\n**오직 다음을 모두 만족할 때만**:\n- **stakes 高** — 되돌리기가 비싸다 / 데이터·외부 시스템에 파괴적이다 / 프로젝트의 *방향 자체*가 갈린다, 그리고\n- **confidence < 0.5**\n\n그 외에는 전부 가정하고 진행한다.\n틀린 가정은 나중에 결과 피드백으로 교정된다 — 그게 설계된 동작이다.\n\"안 물어봄\"의 비용은 \"나중에 고침\"으로 치른다.\n\n### 3. task_type을 분류하라\n`feature_impl | research | harness_build | infra | refactor | investigation` 중 하나.\n이 분류가 executor 선택과 게이트 전략을 결정하므로 정확히.\n\n### 4. 스코프를 능동적으로 잘라라\n`non_goals`를 **최소 2개** 명시한다.\n\"이번엔 안 한다\"를 분명히 하는 것이 드리프트를 막는다.\n\n### 5. done_when — 통합 게이트\n개별 `acceptance_criteria`의 합과 *별개로*,\n\"전체가 합쳐져 원래 주문을 충족하는가\"를 한 줄로 쓴다.\n(보통: 모든 ac 통과 AND 기존 스위트 무회귀)\n\n### 6. 사실은 context에서만\n`constraints`·기존 규약·스택은 `project_context`에 있는 것만 쓴다.\n없으면 비워두거나 assumption으로 처리한다.\n\n### 7. 없는 능력은 요청으로 (capability_requests — 선택)\n빌드가 *context에 없는 외부 능력*(특정 라이브러리/툴 — 예: 경로탐색·물리엔진)을 진짜로\n필요로 하면, **있는 척 가정하지 말고** `capability_requests`에 선언하라(있을 때만, 보수적):\n`capability_requests: [{ capability: \"pathfinding\", unit: u1, reason: \"유닛 이동 경로탐색\" }]`.\n거버넌스가 켜져 있으면 director가 큐레이션 레지스트리에서 후보를 찾아 *사람 승인 후* 채택한다.\n필요 없으면 비워둔다(기본 빈 리스트). 이 필드는 검증 기준이 아니다 — 능력 *요청*일 뿐이다.\n\n---\n\n## 주문이 모호할 때 (중요)\n\n한 줄짜리/막연한 주문은 풍부한 spec을 *지어낼* 근거가 없다.\n억지로 채우면 환각 스코프가 된다. 이때:\n\n- acceptance_criteria를 무리해서 채우지 말고 **minimal viable spec**만 만든다.\n- `decomposition`의 **첫 unit을 \"탐색/조사로 spec을 정제한다\"**로 둔다.\n  (예: 코드베이스·기존 문서 조사 → 무엇을 만들지 구체화)\n- `verifiability`는 잠정값으로 두고 다음 버전에서 올린다.\n\n즉 한 방에 끝내는 게 아니라, **자라날 수 있는 씨앗 spec**을 만든다.\n\n---\n\n## 자기검열 (출력 직전 반드시 통과)\n\n- 모든 ac에 check가 있나?\n- 각 ac의 `unit`이 맞나? 전체-시스템 기준(run 트레이스·end-to-end·교차 유닛)이 기반\n  유닛에 잘못 태그돼 있지 않나? 그런 건 `integration`이어야 한다.\n- 게으른 구현이 ac를 전부 통과하면서도 `goal`을 놓칠 수 있나? 있으면 그 ac를 더 조여라.\n- `non_goals`가 사실 사용자의 *진짜 의도*를 잘라먹고 있지 않나?\n- \"방향이 갈리는\" 가정이 `checkpoint: false`로 숨어 있지 않나?\n\n(이 자기검열은 가벼운 1차 방어선이다. 본격적인 적대적 리뷰는 별도 패스에서 Without this baseline, any path that seeds Food at spawn (e.g. an `Inventory::default()` that is non-empty, or a stray bootstrap write) makes A2 pass with the gather code deleted. Exact == 0 at tick 0 establishes the zero baseline; because eating-from-inventory is out of scope this slice, any Food observed later in A2 therefore can ONLY have come from a gather pickup. This converts A2 from circular to discriminating."\012- ticks: 0 (measured at bootstrap, before run)\012- components_read: [Inventory]\012\012### Assertion 2: Gather pickup is non-vacuous (food actually lands in inventories at population scale)\012- metric: count of full-runtime agents whose `Inventory.get(ResourceKind::Food) >= 1` after the span (query `&Inventory` over live agents; carried food persists because eating-from-inventory is out of scope this slice, so pickups accumulate monotonically)\012- threshold: >= 3 agents carry Food >= 1\012- type: C\012- rationale: "NOTE (corrected per challenge): this assertion proves only that >= 3 inventories CONTAIN food at end-of-span; combined with Assertion 1's tick-0 zero baseline it proves food entered inventories via the gather path during the run, but it does NOT by itself prove the full Idle->Seeking{GatherFood}->Consuming{GatherFood}->Idle cycle resolved cleanly — that mechanism proof lives in Assertions 7/8 (ground->inventory transfer). Assert against the constant by name, not the literal 10, so a future cap change does not silently invalidate the test. CAVEAT (per challenge): this is an OUTCOME guard and can pass vacuously if the near-capacity clamp branch is never exercised in the production run; Assertion 4 forces that branch explicitly."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [Inventory]\012\012### Assertion 4: Overflow clamp branch is exercised (controlled near-capacity fixture)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario, one agent is given inventory room of exactly 1 (`Inventory.total() == INVENTORY_CAPACITY - 1`) and placed adjacent to a single FINITE food tile of known count C >= 3 with all needs satisfied; run until that agent completes exactly one GatherFood pickup. Measure (a) the agent's `Inventory.get(Food)` delta, (b) the tile count delta, (c) whether the tile is removed from `food_tiles`\012- threshold: agent Food delta == 1 (clamped to the 1 unit of room) AND tile count delta == -1 AND the remaining C-1 units STAY on the tile (tile NOT removed, NOT zeroed). Proves the returned-overflow value is honoured: only `room` units transfer, the remainder is returned to the ground (not silently dropped, not over-taken). The 'pickup completes within <= 300 ticks' clause prevents a hardcode-at-spawn implementation from passing by never actually gathering. Exact equalities — conservation must be exact."\012- ticks: bounded <= 300\012- components_read: [Inventory, AgentState, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 5: Inventories hold ONLY Food this slice (Food-only behaviour)\012- metric: sum across all agents of `Inventory.get(Water) + Inventory.get(Wood) + Inventory.get(Stone)` after the span\012- threshold: == 0\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Spec invariant — Section 2 'Out of scope' locks gathering to Food only this slice (`ResourceKind` keeps all four variants but the behaviour is Food-only). Exact == 0, no margin."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [Inventory]\012\012### Assertion 6: Infinite source tiles never deplete (per-tick sentinel preservation — the headline correctness bug)\012- metric: capture the set of `food_tiles` keys whose value == RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE (255) BEFORE the span; then on EVERY tick across the span record, for each originally-infinite key, (a) that the key still exists in `food_tiles` and (b) its value == 255. Report the per-tick minimum value observed over each originally-infinite key\012- threshold: for every originally-infinite key, on every tick across the span: key exists AND value == 255 (per-tick min over each key == 255); the originally-infinite key set count is unchanged at end-of-span\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Invariant flagged in Section 0 as the real bug risk: the naive `*counter -= take` corrupts the 255 sentinel and turns an infinite source finite. make_stage1_engine seeds ~12 infinite tiles, so this is the COMMON path. Per-tick (not endpoint-only) sampling is REQUIRED because `resource_regen` could heal a wrongly-decremented 255 back to 255 before an endpoint capture, masking the corruption — only continuous observation catches a transient dip. If any 255 key drops below 255 on any tick or disappears, a previously-infinite source became finite = corruption. This is the single most important preservation assertion."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [] (SimResources::food_tiles only)\012\012### Assertion 7: Positive pickup from an infinite tile (sentinel TAKE branch is actually executed)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario, one agent with inventory room and all needs satisfied is placed adjacent to a single INFINITE food tile (value == RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE / 255) with NO other eligible tile in range; run until it completes exactly one GatherFood pickup. A6 alone passes vacuously if the implementation simply never targets infinite tiles (dead sentinel branch). A7 FORCES an agent to gather FROM a 255 tile, proving the take-from-infinite branch executes AND leaves the sentinel intact (add to inventory, no decrement). Together A6 (no corruption anywhere) + A7 (the sentinel branch positively runs and is correct) close the sentinel hole completely. The completion-within-bound clause blocks a hardcode-at-spawn pass."\012- ticks: bounded <= 300\012- components_read: [Inventory, AgentState, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 8: Finite tile depletes by exactly the amount picked up (conservation, controlled fixture)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario with a single FINITE food tile of known count C placed adjacent to one satisfied agent with inventory room R, run until that agent completes one GatherFood pickup; measure (tile_count_before - tile_count_after) and the agent's `Inventory.get(Food)` delta\012- threshold: tile decrement == agent Food gain == min(C, R); tile removed from `food_tiles` iff it reached 0; conservation holds exactly (no food created or destroyed). Pickup MUST complete within <= 300 ticks (hard failure if it does not)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Conservation invariant — every unit removed from a finite ground tile must appear in exactly one agent's inventory. Counterpart to A6/A7: proves the finite branch (decrement by amount actually taken) is correct. A controlled fixture is required because make_stage1_engine's seeded tiles are predominantly infinite — relying on the production scene to contain a depletable finite tile risks vacuity. The completion-within-bound clause blocks a no-pickup vacuous pass (0 == min(C,R) would otherwise be falsely satisfiable if nothing moved)."\012- ticks: bounded <= 300\012- components_read: [Inventory, AgentState, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 9: Gather does NOT change Hunger (gather is distinct from eat — paired control)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario, two agents start identical with all needs satisfied and inventory room: Agent G has a reachable food tile (will gather); Agent C has NO reachable food tile in range (will not gather). Run both for the SAME tick count, ending on the tick Agent G completes exactly one GatherFood pickup. Measure `Hunger.value` for both agents at that tick\012- threshold: `Hunger_G == Hunger_C` (bit-identical f64). Precondition: Agent G must have completed >= 1 GatherFood pickup (hard failure if not — otherwise the comparison is vacuous)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Core design invariant from Section 1: gathering carries food into Inventory WITHOUT reducing Hunger (unlike the eat path). CORRECTED per challenge: an absolute `Hunger delta == 0` would FALSE-FAIL because the needs system increments Hunger every tick — that ambient accrual is unrelated to gathering. The paired-control method isolates the gather contribution: G and C accrue identical ambient hunger over identical ticks, so if gathering does not touch Hunger, their Hunger values stay bit-identical. Any divergence means the gather Consuming arm wrote Hunger = the eat/gather behaviours have collapsed. (This also removes the perverse incentive for the Generator to 'fix' a false-failing absolute check by zeroing Hunger in the gather path — the exact bug A9 guards against.)"\012- ticks: bounded <= 600\012- components_read: [Hunger, Inventory, AgentState, Position]\012\012### Assertion 10: Hunger arm preempts Gather (a hungry agent eats, never gathers)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario where one agent has Hunger ABOVE its eat threshold with a food tile nearby and inventory room, observe its AgentState every tick until Hunger drops below threshold: count ticks the agent spent in `Seeking{GatherFood}` or `Consuming{GatherFood}` WHILE Hunger was above threshold; also confirm Hunger decreases (it ate)\012- threshold: gather-state ticks while hungry == 0 AND final Hunger < initial Hunger\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Cascade-priority invariant: arm_priority_index Hunger=0 outranks Gather=6, so a hungry agent must resolve Hunger first and can never select the Gather arm while above the hunger threshold. This makes 'carries food but starves' impossible at the decision level (Section 1). Exact == 0 — a single tick of gathering-while-hungry violates the ordering. The final-Hunger-decreased clause confirms the agent actually ate from a ground tile (the accepted MVP path), not merely idled."\012- ticks: bounded <= 600\012- components_read: [Hunger, AgentState, Inventory, Position]\012\012### Assertion 11: No targetless Seeking (freeze guard — primary, per-tick, settlement-forming fixture)\012- metric: in a scenario that PLACES startup buildings so a settlement actually forms and members are pulled outside proximity to gather (mirroring the production world_renderer building placement, NOT the bare make_stage1_engine which forms no settlement), on EVERY tick across the full span count agents observed in any `AgentState::Seeking { .. }` whose `SeekTarget` is absent/unset\012- threshold: == 0 on every tick. Precondition: at least one settlement must form during the run (hard failure / VACUOUS report if no settlement forms — otherwise this guard does not cover the freeze class it claims)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Invariant + freeze origin (agent_decision.rs). A Seeking state with no SeekTarget is the exact freeze signature this session has chased: movement.rs suppresses Brownian for all Seeking, and with no target the agent never steps and never resolves. Per-tick sampling guarantees a permanent-targetless-Seeking freeze cannot slip between sample points. SCENARIO CORRECTED per challenge: the project's own finding is 'cargo WITHOUT buildings = healthy' — running on bare make_stage1_engine would let this guard pass vacuously while the production scene (agents + buildings -> settlement -> members gather outside proximity) still freezes. The fixture MUST form a settlement so the freeze-triggering condition is actually present."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [AgentState, SeekTarget] (+ SimResources::settlements)\012\012### Assertion 12: No agent stuck in gather states beyond a bounded streak (freeze guard — secondary)\012- metric: in the same settlement-forming fixture as Assertion 11, max consecutive ticks any single agent remains continuously in `Seeking{GatherFood}` OR `Consuming{GatherFood}` (per-agent frozen-streak counter, same pattern as the p10 / membership A10(a)/(b) streak guards)\012- threshold: <= 200 ticks\012- type: D\012- rationale: "Regression guard for the agent_decision freeze class. The failure mode is PERMANENT stuck (streak in the thousands, ~= span length 4380), not a tight per-cycle estimate. GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS=8 Chebyshev bounds travel-to-tile and Consuming is a single-tick unconditional exit to Idle, so a healthy gather cycle resolves far below 200; 200 is calibrated against the permanent-stuck failure with wide headroom for slow movement / re-seek churn, NOT presented as a tight bound. Generator MUST record the observed max streak; if it approaches 200, the Consuming exit may not be unconditional — investigate before locking."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [AgentState, SeekTarget]\012\012### Assertion 13: Full-inventory agent does not re-select Gather (busy-loop guard)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario, one agent at `Inventory.total() == INVENTORY_CAPACITY` (full) with all needs satisfied is placed adjacent to a food tile; observe its AgentState every tick over a bounded window and count ticks spent in `Seeking{GatherFood}` or `Consuming{GatherFood}`\012- threshold: gather-state ticks == 0 (a full agent never selects the Gather arm)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Busy-loop / freeze-adjacent guard demanded by the challenge edge-case review. A full inventory has no room, so the Gather arm's precondition must fail and the agent must fall through to a lower-priority arm — it must NOT enter Seeking{GatherFood} and spin. Exact == 0; any gather-state tick for a full agent is a re-selection bug that risks a stuck/oscillating state."\012- ticks: bounded <= 300\012- components_read: [AgentState, Inventory, Position]\012\012### Assertion 14: Member persistence under gathering (membership-collapse regression guard)\012- metric: in the settlement-forming fixture (same class as Assertion 11), across the span track members that travel OUTSIDE their settlement's proximity radius while gathering. PART A (precondition): count distinct members observed at least once OUTSIDE proximity during the span. PART B (the guard): for every member observed outside proximity, confirm it still appears in that settlement's `member_agents` (count violations = members dropped while outside proximity for a non-death/non-migration reason)\012- threshold: PART A: >= 3 distinct members observed outside proximity at some point (if < 3, the test is VACUOUS — Generator MUST report and investigate, NOT silently pass). PART B: violations == 0\012- type: D\012- rationale: "Regression guard for the exact bug that killed the prior 2-2 attempt (75->0 member collapse): gathering pulled members outside the proximity bubble and the old per-tick proximity recompute dropped them. The membership reform (256322dd) replaced this with a belonging model — members leave only on death/migration. PART A closes the vacuity hole the challenge flagged: if no member ever leaves proximity (food inside settlements, or no settlement forms), PART B passes trivially and catches nothing — so PART A REQUIRES >= 3 members to actually venture out before PART B's invariant is meaningful. If PART A fails the fixture is inadequate; if PART B fails, STOP and report per Section 6 (do not auto-proceed) — the membership regression has re-emerged."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [AgentState, Position] (+ SimResources::settlements.member_agents)\012\012### Assertion 15: Lockstep determinism\012- metric: two independent `make_stage1_engine(42, 20)` runs of identical tick count; compare a canonical state digest = (sorted agent positions) + (per-agent Food inventory counts) + (sorted `food_tiles` map)\012- threshold: digest_run_a == digest_run_b (byte-identical)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Determinism invariant. Section 3 commits to nearest-tile tie-break (dist, x, y), BTreeMap-ordered Inventory, and reuse of the existing same-tick resource snapshot — i.e. no new HashMap iteration on the decision hot path. Any nondeterminism (e.g. HashMap iteration order leaking into target choice) breaks lockstep. Including food_tiles in the digest catches order-dependent pickup races. NOTE: lockstep detects NONDETERMINISTIC divergence; it does NOT catch a deterministic double-spend (two agents draining one tile below conservation in a repeatable way) — that gap is recorded in Edge Cases / NOT in Scope."\012- ticks: 2000 (long enough that gathering has occurred in both runs; short enough to keep the dual run cheap)\012- components_read: [Inventory, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 16: suppresses_movement FSM correctness (unit, agent_state.rs)\012- metric: `AgentState::Seeking { target: TargetKind::GatherFood }.suppresses_movement()` and `AgentState::Consuming { target: TargetKind::GatherFood }.suppresses_movement()`\012- threshold: Seeking{GatherFood} -> true; Consuming{GatherFood} -> false\012- type: A\012- rationale: "FSM invariant per Section 2. All Seeking suppress movement (blanket matches! rule) so the agent walks toward its target only via SeekTarget-driven movement, not Brownian; Consuming{GatherFood} must NOT suppress (mirrors Food/Water/Sleep/ConstructionSite) because the decision system owns the Consuming-tick exit. These two booleans are the contract that keeps the gather cycle from freezing. Pure unit assertion — no engine ticks."\012- ticks: 0 (unit test in agent_state.rs)\012- components_read: [AgentState]\012\012## Edge Cases\012- Tile emptied by another agent before arrival: pickup takes 0, agent transitions to Idle the same tick (no re-seek loop, no stuck Consuming). Covered behaviourally by Assertions 11 + 12 (unconditional exit means even a 0-pickup ends in Idle).\012- Inventory full on arrival (total() == INVENTORY_CAPACITY): agent does not select/enter Gather at all — dedicated guard is Assertion 13 (gather-state ticks == 0); freeze-adjacency also covered by Assertion 12.\012- Overflow on partial room (room < tile count): only `room` units transfer, remainder stays on the tile — dedicated guard is Assertion 4.\012- tile_count == room exactly (equality boundary): bracketed by Assertion 8 (R >= C path) and Assertion 4 (R < C path). Generator MAY add an explicit equality fixture if cheap, but it is NOT a required threshold.\012- Infinite source tile under pickup: ADD to inventory, NO decrement, tile NOT removed (the common case). Assertion 6 (no corruption, per-tick) + Assertion 7 (positive take from 255) are the dedicated guards.\012- No eligible food tile within GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS: Gather arm does not fire; agent falls through to the Settlement arm. Negative path — verified implicitly by absence of targetless Seeking in Assertion 11.\012- Two agents targeting the SAME finite tile in the same tick (double-spend / negative count): NOT directly asserted this slice. A8 is single-agent conservation; A15's digest detects nondeterministic races but not a deterministic double-spend. This is a KNOWN coverage gap recorded here (not silently dropped) and deferred to a follow-up multi-agent contention fixture — see NOT in Scope.\012- Production-scene tile depletion link: production tile depletion is NOT asserted directly, because make_stage1_engine seeds predominantly INFINITE tiles (Assertion 6 confirms these never deplete). Real ground->inventory depletion of FINITE tiles is proven only by the controlled fixtures Assertions 4 and 8. This is intentional — the production scene contains few/no depletable finite food tiles, so a production-scale finite-depletion assertion would be vacuous.\012- Existing cross-phase suite MUST stay green (regression guards, permitted by scope rule 4): `harness_settlements_zero_regression`, `harness_membership_belonging`, `harness_settlement_migration_unfreeze`, `harness_s16_zeta_social_freeze_fix`, `harness_p10_alpha/beta/gamma`. Any failure in these = STOP + report (do not auto-fix), since they signal the membership/freeze regression has re-emerged.\012- Scope-gap check: Section 2 manifest is sufficient for this plan — all assertions read existing fields (Inventory from slice 2-1, food_tiles/settlements/AgentState/SeekTarget already present). No omission identified beyond the authorized `TargetKind::GatherFood`, `CascadeArm::Gather`, `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS`, and the `Inventory::default()` spawn-site attachments.\012\012## Visual Verification Hints\012- If gathering works, in a windowed run agents away from their settlement should still be carrying/holding food state, and ground food markers on FINITE tiles should visibly decrement over time while INFINITE source tiles (the seeded ~12) stay constant — no infinite source should ever visibly empty.\012- If the freeze regression returned, agents would be visibly frozen mid-map (no Brownian jitter) shortly after a settlement forms — the exact mass-freeze symptom from this session. Healthy behaviour: members leave the settlement bubble, gather, and return without locking up.\012- What would look WRONG: an infinite source tile depleting to empty (sentinel corruption); agents stacking food beyond the capacity cap; a hungry agent walking to a food tile to gather (instead of eating) and starving while carrying food.\012\012## NOT in Scope\012- Storing carried `Inventory` into `Settlement::stockpile` (slice 2-3) — not asserted.\012- Eating from one's own `Inventory`/stockpile when hungry (slice 2-4) — Assertion 10 deliberately confirms a hungry agent eats from GROUND tiles, the accepted MVP limit; the plan does NOT check inventory-eating.\012- Gathering Water / Wood / Stone — Assertion 5 actively forbids non-Food pickup this slice.\012- Two-agent / N-agent contention on a single finite tile (deterministic double-spend, negative tile count): KNOWN coverage gap, explicitly deferred to a follow-up multi-agent fixture. A8 covers single-agent conservation; A15 covers nondeterminism. Recorded, not silently dropped.\012- EventBus `ResourceGathered`, SimBridge inventory getter, GDScript inventory/carry/depletion visualisation (slice 2-5) — no FFI or UI assertions (the Visual Verification Hints above are observational guidance only, not asserted thresholds).\012- `MemoryArm::Gather` / memory-biased gathering — Section 2 confirms `cascade_arm_memory_tag(Gather) -> None`; the plan does not test memory-weight behaviour for the Gather arm (intentionally kept out of Fix-D nondeterminism territory).\012- Absolute resource-balance tuning (starvation-death rate, settlement-count drift vs pre-2-2 baseline) — observational per Section 6, NOT asserted with hard thresholds; only the non-collapse regression guards (Assertions 6, 7, 14 + existing suite) are enforced.\012\012## Feature to Implement\012\012# Direction-2 slice 2-2 — Gather pickup (ground food → Inventory)\012\012> Repo: hyunlord/new-world · Branch: lead/main · base HEAD: 0612b872\012\012## Section 0: Orchestrator Step-0 findings (READ FIRST — de-risks the one-pass requirement)\012\012These are verified facts from a codebase sweep done before this run. Use them; do not\012re-derive incorrectly.\012\012**Stash decision (precondition 1): FRESH — do NOT `stash pop`.** `git stash@{0}`\012("2-2-gather-pickup-WIP") predates the membership reform (256322dd) and ~20 later commits;\012its 170-line `agent_decision.rs` diff is against a defunct base (heavy conflicts), it includes\012out-of-scope FFI (`world_node.rs` = slice 2-5 viz), and it embodies the membership-regressing\012approach. The stash stays intact as historical reference; implement fresh per Sections 2–3.\012The stash's `GatherFood` doc-comment is a fine reference (reproduced below).\012\012**Inventory API (slice 2-1, `sim-core/src/components/inventory.rs`) — confirmed signatures:**\012- `pub const INVENTORY_CAPACITY: u32 = 10;`\012- `pub fn total(&self) -> u32`\012- `pub fn get(&self, kind: ResourceKind) -> u32`\012- `pub fn add(&mut self, kind: ResourceKind, n: u32) -> u32` — adds up to remaining capacity,\012  **returns the overflow** (amount NOT added). Section 3's pseudocode matches this exactly.\012\012**`food_tiles` type + INFINITE-SOURCE SENTINEL (critical — Section 3 pseudocode is incomplete here):**\012- `food_tiles: HashMap<(u32, u32), u8>` (in `sim-engine/src/lib.rs:187`).\012- **`RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE = 255`**: a tile at this sentinel NEVER depletes. The eat path\012  (agent_decision.rs ~L1210) guards it: `if *counter != RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE { *counter =\012  counter.saturating_sub(1); … }`. **The Gather pickup MUST mirror this guard** — on an infinite\012  tile, ADD to inventory but do NOT decrement and do NOT remove the tile. The\012  `make_stage1_engine`/production scene seeds ~12 infinite source tiles, so this is the COMMON\012  case, not an edge case. Naively doing `*counter -= take` (as Section 3's simplified pseudocode\012  shows) would corrupt the 255 sentinel and turn an infinite source finite — a real bug. Correct\012  Gather pickup:\012  ```rust\012  let want = INVENTORY_CAPACITY.saturating_sub(inventory.total());\012  if want > 0 {\012      if let Some(counter) = resources.food_tiles.get_mut(&key) {\012          if *counter == RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE {\012              inventory.add(ResourceKind::Food, want);      // infinite: take freely, no decrement\012          } else {\012              let take = (*counter as u32).min(want);\012              if take > 0 {\012                  let overflow = inventory.add(ResourceKind::Food, take);\012                  let actually = take - overflow;            // overflow == 0 here (want ≤ room)\012                  *counter = counter.saturating_sub(actually as u8);\012                  if *counter == 0 { resources.food_tiles.remove(&key); }\012              }\012          }\012      }\012  }\012  *state = AgentState::Idle;   // UNCONDITIONAL — even if tile gone / room 0 / infinite\012  ```\012\012**Exhaustive `match target` sites a new `TargetKind::GatherFood` variant must extend\012(both in agent_decision.rs — compiler enforces, but pre-listed to keep it one-pass):**\0121. ~L1078 `has_resource` (the Seeking-reached → Consuming edge): add\012   `TargetKind::GatherFood => resources.food_tiles.get(&key).copied().is_some_and(|v| v > 0)`\012   (tile reached iff food present — same as `Food`).\0122. ~L1205 the consume match: add the `TargetKind::GatherFood => { …pickup… *state = Idle }` arm\012   above using the infinite-sentinel-safe logic.\012   Any other non-exhaustive match the compiler flags is in-scope to fix in the same pass.\012\012**`suppresses_movement` (agent_state.rs ~L94-107):** `Seeking` is a blanket\012`matches!(self, AgentState::Seeking { .. })` → `Seeking{GatherFood}` = true automatically.\012`Consuming` returns true ONLY for `Consuming { target: TargetKind::Agent(_) }` (explicit);\012everything else Consuming → false → `Consuming{GatherFood}` = false automatically. Spec-correct\012as-is; the unit tests must still assert both explicitly (Section 2).\012\012**Stash `GatherFood` doc-comment (reference, reusable):**\012```rust\012/// Direction-2 slice 2-2 — pick up Ground food into [`Inventory`] (carry,\012/// NOT eat). `Consuming { GatherFood }` is a PICKUP, not a consume: it adds Food\012/// to the agent's `Inventory` and decrements the food tile, but reduces NO need.\012/// Distinct from [`TargetKind::Food`], which is eaten (reduces Hunger). Selected\012/// by the lowest-but-one priority `CascadeArm::Gather` (index 6) for settled,\012/// well-fed members with inventory room.\012```\012\012**`decision/mod.rs` re-exports:** add `arm_priority_index, cascade_arm_memory_tag, CascadeArm`\012to the `pub use agent_decision::{…}` list if the new harness test references them (the stash did).\012\012---\012\012## Execution Directive\012\012Implement the Gather pickup behaviour: a satisfied agent walks to nearby ground food and\012picks it up into its `Inventory` (carrying, NOT eating). This is the runtime that slice 2-1's\012data structures were built for. agent_decision.rs is the freeze origin of this session's bugs\012— the Consuming exit MUST be unconditional (always → Idle), exactly like the existing Food\012consume guard. A NEW harness test plus the freeze/regression suite are mandatory, all\012assertions implemented in one pass (no incremental RE-CODE loop).\012\012---\012\012## Section 1: Implementation Intent\012\012Agents can satisfy hunger by eating ground food, but they cannot yet *carry* food. Without\012carrying, there is no provisioning, no stockpiling, and no resource economy — settlements are\012just co-located eaters. Slice 2-1 shipped the carry/store data (`Inventory`,\012`Settlement::stockpile`, `ResourceKind`) as pure structures with no runtime. Slice 2-2 adds\012the first runtime that fills an `Inventory`: opportunistic gathering.\012\012Design (state explicitly so it can be vetoed):\012- **Gather is distinct from eating.** Eating (`Hunger` arm) consumes a food tile to reduce\012  `Hunger`. Gathering picks food off the ground into the agent's `Inventory` without changing\012  `Hunger`. Distinct outcome ⇒ distinct FSM target: a new `TargetKind::GatherFood`, mirroring\012  the Phase 5-γ/6-α precedent of adding a `TargetKind` variant (NOT a new `AgentState` variant).\012- **Gather only when survival is handled.** A new lowest-but-one cascade arm `CascadeArm::Gather`\012  at priority index 6, pushing `Settlement` to 7. Because `Hunger`(0)/`Thirst`(1)/`Fatigue`(2)\012  outrank it, a hungry agent always eats first; only a satisfied agent with inventory room\012  gathers. This makes "carries food but starves" impossible at the decision level — the higher\012  arms preempt gathering the moment a need crosses threshold.\012- **Why this is now safe (the prior regression is structurally gone).** The earlier 2-2 attempt\012  collapsed settlements: gathering pulled members outside the proximity bubble and the old\012  per-tick proximity recompute dropped them (75→0 members). The membership reform (256322dd)\012  replaced that with a belonging model — members persist outside proximity, leaving only on\012  death/migration — so a member gathering far away stays a member. The regression's root cause\012  no longer exists; the freeze/regression harness must confirm this empirically.\012- **Bounded scan for efficiency and gameplay.** Gathering searches food tiles only within a\012  Chebyshev radius `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS`, not the whole map — keeps the decision hot-path bounded\012  (Gate 6) and keeps satisfied agents from trekking across the world for low-value pickups.\012- **No memory bias.** Like `Settlement`, `CascadeArm::Gather` maps to no `MemoryArm` (positional/\012  opportunistic arm). This deliberately keeps Gather out of the nondeterminism-sensitive\012  memory-weight machinery (Fix-D territory) — no `MemoryArm` enum change.\012\012---\012\012## Section 2: What to Build\012\012### sim-core (`rust/crates/sim-core/src/components/agent_state.rs`)\012- Add `TargetKind::GatherFood` (a new variant after `Agent(AgentId)`; non-payload, like\012  Food/Water/Sleep/ConstructionSite). Document it as the carry-pickup target distinct from\012  the eat target `Food`.\012- Update `AgentState::suppresses_movement`:\012  - `Seeking { target: GatherFood }` → **true** (all `Seeking` suppress — unchanged rule, the\012    existing `matches!(self, AgentState::Seeking { .. })` already covers it; verify it does).\012  - `Consuming { target: GatherFood }` → **false** (like Food/Water/Sleep/ConstructionSite; the\012    decision system owns its own Consuming-tick exit, so no Brownian suppression).\012  - Extend the unit tests in that file to assert both.\012- No new `AgentState` variant. No `MemoryArm` change.\012\012### sim-systems (`rust/crates/sim-systems/src/runtime/decision/agent_decision.rs`)\012- `enum CascadeArm`: add `Gather` between `Combat` and `Settlement`.\012- `arm_priority_index`: `Hunger=0, Thirst=1, Fatigue=2, Construction=3, Social=4, Combat=5,\012  Gather=6, Settlement=7`.\012- `cascade_arm_memory_tag(CascadeArm::Gather)` → `None`.\012- New decision constants (place beside the existing `HUNGER_THRESHOLD` / consume-amount module\012  constants — match their current location and typing):\012  - `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS: u32` — Chebyshev radius around the agent within which a ground-food\012    tile is eligible for gathering. Default `8` (tune via harness; document the rationale).\012- Gather arm logic in the Idle cascade (see Section 3): trigger condition, target selection,\012  Seeking→Consuming→Idle transitions, pickup math.\012\012### Agent spawn sites — attach `Inventory`\012- Every site that spawns a *full runtime agent* must include `Inventory::default()` in its\012  component tuple. Idle agents evaluate cascade arms in\012priority order; the first satisfied arm sets the `Seeking` target. Gather is evaluated only\012after Hunger/Thirst/Fatigue/Construction/Social/Combat are all ruled out, and before Settlement.\012\012**Trigger (Idle → Seeking{GatherFood}).** Fire the Gather arm when ALL hold:\0121. The agent is `Idle` and no higher-priority arm fired this tick (i.e. survival + social +\012   combat are not active — Hunger ≤ its threshold, Thirst ≤ its threshold, Fatigue ≤ its\012   threshold, no construction/social/combat target).\0122. `inventory.total() < INVENTORY_CAPACITY` (room to carry; `INVENTORY_CAPACITY = 10`).\0123. There exists a ground food tile (`SimResources::food_tiles`, count > 0) within\012   `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS` Chebyshev of the agent's `Position`.\012If so → `AgentState::Seeking { target: TargetKind::GatherFood }` with the chosen tile as the\012`SeekTarget`. No re-seek loop, no stuck Consuming. If any 255 key drops below 255 on any tick or disappears, a previously-infinite source became finite = corruption. This is the single most important preservation assertion."\012- ticks: 4380\012- components_read: [] (SimResources::food_tiles only)\012\012### Assertion 7: Positive pickup from an infinite tile (sentinel TAKE branch is actually executed)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario, one agent with inventory room and all needs satisfied is placed adjacent to a single INFINITE food tile (value == RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE / 255) with NO other eligible tile in range; run until it completes exactly one GatherFood pickup. Measure (a) the agent's `Inventory.get(Food)` delta and (b) the tile's value immediately after the pickup tick\012- threshold: agent Food delta >= 1 (pickup succeeded) AND the tile value is still == 255 after the pickup. Pickup MUST complete within <= 300 ticks (hard failure if it does not)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "The missing partner to Assertion 6 demanded by the challenge. A6 alone passes vacuously if the implementation simply never targets infinite tiles (dead sentinel branch). A7 FORCES an agent to gather FROM a 255 tile, proving the take-from-infinite branch executes AND leaves the sentinel intact (add to inventory, no decrement). Together A6 (no corruption anywhere) + A7 (the sentinel branch positively runs and is correct) close the sentinel hole completely. The completion-within-bound clause blocks a hardcode-at-spawn pass."\012- ticks: bounded <= 300\012- components_read: [Inventory, AgentState, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 8: Finite tile depletes by exactly the amount picked up (conservation, controlled fixture)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario with a single FINITE food tile of known count C placed adjacent to one satisfied agent with inventory room R, run until that agent completes one GatherFood pickup; measure (tile_count_before - tile_count_after) and the agent's `Inventory.get(Food)` delta\012- threshold: tile decrement == agent Food gain == min(C, R); tile removed from `food_tiles` iff it reached 0; conservation holds exactly (no food created or destroyed). Counterpart to A6/A7: proves the finite branch (decrement by amount actually taken) is correct. A controlled fixture is required because make_stage1_engine's seeded tiles are predominantly infinite — relying on the production scene to contain a depletable finite tile risks vacuity. The completion-within-bound clause blocks a no-pickup vacuous pass (0 == min(C,R) would otherwise be falsely satisfiable if nothing moved)."\012- ticks: bounded <= 300\012- components_read: [Inventory, AgentState, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 9: Gather does NOT change Hunger (gather is distinct from eat — paired control)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario, two agents start identical with all needs satisfied and inventory room: Agent G has a reachable food tile (will gather); Agent C has NO reachable food tile in range (will not gather). Run both for the SAME tick count, ending on the tick Agent G completes exactly one GatherFood pickup. Measure `Hunger.value` for both agents at that tick\012- threshold: `Hunger_G == Hunger_C` (bit-identical f64). Precondition: Agent G must have completed >= 1 GatherFood pickup (hard failure if not — otherwise the comparison is vacuous)\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Core design invariant from Section 1: gathering carries food into Inventory WITHOUT reducing Hunger (unlike the eat path). CORRECTED per challenge: an absolute `Hunger delta == 0` would FALSE-FAIL because the needs system increments Hunger every tick — that ambient accrual is unrelated to gathering. The paired-control method isolates the gather contribution: G and C accrue identical ambient hunger over identical ticks, so if gathering does not touch Hunger, their Hunger values stay bit-identical. Any divergence means the gather Consuming arm wrote Hunger = the eat/gather behaviours have collapsed. (This also removes the perverse incentive for the Generator to 'fix' a false-failing absolute check by zeroing Hunger in the gather path — the exact bug A9 guards against.)"\012- ticks: bounded <= 600\012- components_read: [Hunger, Inventory, AgentState, Position]\012\012### Assertion 10: Hunger arm preempts Gather (a hungry agent eats, never gathers)\012- metric: in a controlled scenario where one agent has Hunger ABOVE its eat threshold with a food tile nearby and inventory room, observe its AgentState every tick until Hunger drops below threshold: count ticks the agent spent in `Seeking{GatherFood}` or `Consuming{GatherFood}` WHILE Hunger was above threshold; also confirm Hunger decreases (it ate)\012- threshold: gather-state ticks while hungry == 0 AND final Hunger < initial Hunger\012- type: A\012- rationale: "Cascade-priority invariant: arm_priority_index Hunger=0 outranks Gather=6, so a hungry agent must resolve Hunger first and can never select the Gather arm while above the hunger threshold. This makes 'carries food but starves' impossible at the decision level (Section 1). Exact == 0 — a single tick of gathering-while-hungry violates the ordering. The final-Hunger-decreased clause confirms the agent actually ate from a ground tile (the accepted MVP path), not merely idled."\012- ticks: bounded <= 600\012- components_read: [Hunger, AgentState, Inventory, Position]\012\012### Assertion 11: No targetless Seeking (freeze guard — primary, per-tick, settlement-forming fixture)\012- metric: in a scenario that PLACES startup buildings so a settlement actually forms and members are pulled outside proximity to gather (mirroring the production world_renderer building placement, NOT the bare make_stage1_engine which forms no settlement), on EVERY tick across the full span count agents observed in any `AgentState::Seeking { .. }` whose `SeekTarget` is absent/unset\012- threshold: == 0 on every tick. Any nondeterminism (e.g. HashMap iteration order leaking into target choice) breaks lockstep. Including food_tiles in the digest catches order-dependent pickup races. NOTE: lockstep detects NONDETERMINISTIC divergence; it does NOT catch a deterministic double-spend (two agents draining one tile below conservation in a repeatable way) — that gap is recorded in Edge Cases / NOT in Scope."\012- ticks: 2000 (long enough that gathering has occurred in both runs; short enough to keep the dual run cheap)\012- components_read: [Inventory, Position] (+ SimResources::food_tiles)\012\012### Assertion 16: suppresses_movement FSM correctness (unit, agent_state.rs)\012- metric: `AgentState::Seeking { target: TargetKind::GatherFood }.suppresses_movement()` and `AgentState::Consuming { target: TargetKind::GatherFood }.suppresses_movement()`\012- threshold: Seeking{GatherFood} -> true; Consuming{GatherFood} -> false\012- type: A\012- rationale: "FSM invariant per Section 2. These two booleans are the contract that keeps the gather cycle from freezing. Pure unit assertion — no engine ticks."\012- ticks: 0 (unit test in agent_state.rs)\012- components_read: [AgentState]\012\012## Edge Cases\012- Tile emptied by another agent before arrival: pickup takes 0, agent transitions to Idle the same tick (no re-seek loop, no stuck Consuming). Covered behaviourally by Assertions 11 + 12 (unconditional exit means even a 0-pickup ends in Idle).\012- Inventory full on arrival (total() == INVENTORY_CAPACITY): agent does not select/enter Gather at all — dedicated guard is Assertion 13 (gather-state ticks == 0); freeze-adjacency also covered by Assertion 12.\012- Overflow on partial room (room < tile count): only `room` units transfer, remainder stays on the tile — dedicated guard is Assertion 4.\012- tile_count == room exactly (equality boundary): bracketed by Assertion 8 (R >= C path) and Assertion 4 (R < C path). Generator MAY add an explicit equality fixture if cheap, but it is NOT a required threshold.\012- Infinite source tile under pickup: ADD to inventory, NO decrement, tile NOT removed (the common case). Assertion 6 (no corruption, per-tick) + Assertion 7 (positive take from 255) are the dedicated guards.\012- No eligible food tile within GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS: Gather arm does not fire; agent falls through to the Settlement arm. Negative path — verified implicitly by absence of targetless Seeking in Assertion 11.\012- Two agents targeting the SAME finite tile in the same tick (double-spend / negative count): NOT directly asserted this slice. A8 is single-agent conservation; A15's digest detects nondeterministic races but not a deterministic double-spend. This is a KNOWN coverage gap recorded here (not silently dropped) and deferred to a follow-up multi-agent contention fixture — see NOT in Scope.\012- Production-scene tile depletion link: production tile depletion is NOT asserted directly, because make_stage1_engine seeds predominantly INFINITE tiles (Assertion 6 confirms these never deplete). Real ground->inventory depletion of FINITE tiles is proven only by the controlled fixtures Assertions 4 and 8. This is intentional — the production scene contains few/no depletable finite food tiles, so a production-scale finite-depletion assertion would be vacuous.\012- Existing cross-phase suite MUST stay green (regression guards, permitted by scope rule 4): `harness_settlements_zero_regression`, `harness_membership_belonging`, `harness_settlement_migration_unfreeze`, `harness_s16_zeta_social_freeze_fix`, `harness_p10_alpha/beta/gamma`. Any failure in these = STOP + report (do not auto-fix), since they signal the membership/freeze regression has re-emerged.\012- Scope-gap check: Section 2 manifest is sufficient for this plan — all assertions read existing fields (Inventory from slice 2-1, food_tiles/settlements/AgentState/SeekTarget already present). No omission identified beyond the authorized `TargetKind::GatherFood`, `CascadeArm::Gather`, `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS`, and the `Inventory::default()` spawn-site attachments.\012\012## Visual Verification Hints\012- If gathering works, in a windowed run agents away from their settlement should still be carrying/holding food state, and ground food markers on FINITE tiles should visibly decrement over time while INFINITE source tiles (the seeded ~12) stay constant — no infinite source should ever visibly empty.\012- If the freeze regression returned, agents would be visibly frozen mid-map (no Brownian jitter) shortly after a settlement forms — the exact mass-freeze symptom from this session. Use them; do not\012re-derive incorrectly.\012\012**Stash decision (precondition 1): FRESH — do NOT `stash pop`.** `git stash@{0}`\012("2-2-gather-pickup-WIP") predates the membership reform (256322dd) and ~20 later commits;\012its 170-line `agent_decision.rs` diff is against a defunct base (heavy conflicts), it includes\012out-of-scope FFI (`world_node.rs` = slice 2-5 viz), and it embodies the membership-regressing\012approach. The stash stays intact as historical reference; implement fresh per Sections 2–3.\012The stash's `GatherFood` doc-comment is a fine reference (reproduced below).\012\012**Inventory API (slice 2-1, `sim-core/src/components/inventory.rs`) — confirmed signatures:**\012- `pub const INVENTORY_CAPACITY: u32 = 10;`\012- `pub fn total(&self) -> u32`\012- `pub fn get(&self, kind: ResourceKind) -> u32`\012- `pub fn add(&mut self, kind: ResourceKind, n: u32) -> u32` — adds up to remaining capacity,\012  **returns the overflow** (amount NOT added). Section 3's pseudocode matches this exactly.\012\012**`food_tiles` type + INFINITE-SOURCE SENTINEL (critical — Section 3 pseudocode is incomplete here):**\012- `food_tiles: HashMap<(u32, u32), u8>` (in `sim-engine/src/lib.rs:187`).\012- **`RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE = 255`**: a tile at this sentinel NEVER depletes. The eat path\012  (agent_decision.rs ~L1210) guards it: `if *counter != RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE { *counter =\012  counter.saturating_sub(1); … }`. **The Gather pickup MUST mirror this guard** — on an infinite\012  tile, ADD to inventory but do NOT decrement and do NOT remove the tile. The\012  `make_stage1_engine`/production scene seeds ~12 infinite source tiles, so this is the COMMON\012  case, not an edge case. Naively doing `*counter -= take` (as Section 3's simplified pseudocode\012  shows) would corrupt the 255 sentinel and turn an infinite source finite — a real bug. Correct\012  Gather pickup:\012  ```rust\012  let want = INVENTORY_CAPACITY.saturating_sub(inventory.total());\012  if want > 0 {\012      if let Some(counter) = resources.food_tiles.get_mut(&key) {\012          if *counter == RESOURCE_SOURCE_INFINITE {\012              inventory.add(ResourceKind::Food, want);      // infinite: take freely, no decrement\012          } else {\012              let take = (*counter as u32).min(want);\012              if take > 0 {\012                  let overflow = inventory.add(ResourceKind::Food, take);\012                  let actually = take - overflow;            // overflow == 0 here (want ≤ room)\012                  *counter = counter.saturating_sub(actually as u8);\012                  if *counter == 0 { resources.food_tiles.remove(&key); }\012              }\012          }\012      }\012  }\012  *state = AgentState::Idle;   // UNCONDITIONAL — even if tile gone / room 0 / infinite\012  ```\012\012**Exhaustive `match target` sites a new `TargetKind::GatherFood` variant must extend\012(both in agent_decision.rs — compiler enforces, but pre-listed to keep it one-pass):**\0121. ~L1078 `has_resource` (the Seeking-reached → Consuming edge): add\012   `TargetKind::GatherFood => resources.food_tiles.get(&key).copied().is_some_and(|v| v > 0)`\012   (tile reached iff food present — same as `Food`).\0122. ~L1205 the consume match: add the `TargetKind::GatherFood => { …pickup… *state = Idle }` arm\012   above using the infinite-sentinel-safe logic.\012   Any other non-exhaustive match the compiler flags is in-scope to fix in the same pass.\012\012**`suppresses_movement` (agent_state.rs ~L94-107):** `Seeking` is a blanket\012`matches!(self, AgentState::Seeking { .. })` → `Seeking{GatherFood}` = true automatically.\012`Consuming` returns true ONLY for `Consuming { target: TargetKind::Agent(_) }` (explicit);\012everything else Consuming → false → `Consuming{GatherFood}` = false automatically. Spec-correct\012as-is; the unit tests must still assert both explicitly (Section 2).\012\012**Stash `GatherFood` doc-comment (reference, reusable):**\012```rust\012/// Direction-2 slice 2-2 — pick up Ground food into [`Inventory`] (carry,\012/// NOT eat). `Consuming { GatherFood }` is a PICKUP, not a consume: it adds Food\012/// to the agent's `Inventory` and decrements the food tile, but reduces NO need.\012/// Distinct from [`TargetKind::Food`], which is eaten (reduces Hunger). Selected\012/// by the lowest-but-one priority `CascadeArm::Gather` (index 6) for settled,\012/// well-fed members with inventory room.\012```\012\012**`decision/mod.rs` re-exports:** add `arm_priority_index, cascade_arm_memory_tag, CascadeArm`\012to the `pub use agent_decision::{…}` list if the new harness test references them (the stash did).\012\012---\012\012## Execution Directive\012\012Implement the Gather pickup behaviour: a satisfied agent walks to nearby ground food and\012picks it up into its `Inventory` (carrying, NOT eating). This is the runtime that slice 2-1's\012data structures were built for. agent_decision.rs is the freeze origin of this session's bugs\012— the Consuming exit MUST be unconditional (always → Idle), exactly like the existing Food\012consume guard. A NEW harness test plus the freeze/regression suite are mandatory, all\012assertions implemented in one pass (no incremental RE-CODE loop).\012\012---\012\012## Section 1: Implementation Intent\012\012Agents can satisfy hunger by eating ground food, but they cannot yet *carry* food. Without\012carrying, there is no provisioning, no stockpiling, and no resource economy — settlements are\012just co-located eaters. Slice 2-1 shipped the carry/store data (`Inventory`,\012`Settlement::stockpile`, `ResourceKind`) as pure structures with no runtime. Slice 2-2 adds\012the first runtime that fills an `Inventory`: opportunistic gathering.\012\012Design (state explicitly so it can be vetoed):\012- **Gather is distinct from eating.** Eating (`Hunger` arm) consumes a food tile to reduce\012  `Hunger`. Gathering picks food off the ground into the agent's `Inventory` without changing\012  `Hunger`. Distinct outcome ⇒ distinct FSM target: a new `TargetKind::GatherFood`, mirroring\012  the Phase 5-γ/6-α precedent of adding a `TargetKind` variant (NOT a new `AgentState` variant).\012- **Gather only when survival is handled.** A new lowest-but-one cascade arm `CascadeArm::Gather`\012  at priority index 6, pushing `Settlement` to 7. Because `Hunger`(0)/`Thirst`(1)/`Fatigue`(2)\012  outrank it, a hungry agent always eats first; only a satisfied agent with inventory room\012  gathers. This makes "carries food but starves" impossible at the decision level — the higher\012  arms preempt gathering the moment a need crosses threshold.\012- **Why this is now safe (the prior regression is structurally gone).** The earlier 2-2 attempt\012  collapsed settlements: gathering pulled members outside the proximity bubble and the old\012  per-tick proximity recompute dropped them (75→0 members). The membership reform (256322dd)\012  replaced that with a belonging model — members persist outside proximity, leaving only on\012  death/migration — so a member gathering far away stays a member. The regression's root cause\012  no longer exists; the freeze/regression harness must confirm this empirically.\012- **Bounded scan for efficiency and gameplay.** Gathering searches food tiles only within a\012  Chebyshev radius `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS`, not the whole map — keeps the decision hot-path bounded\012  (Gate 6) and keeps satisfied agents from trekking across the world for low-value pickups.\012- **No memory bias.** Like `Settlement`, `CascadeArm::Gather` maps to no `MemoryArm` (positional/\012  opportunistic arm). This deliberately keeps Gather out of the nondeterminism-sensitive\012  memory-weight machinery (Fix-D territory) — no `MemoryArm` enum change.\012\012---\012\012## Section 2: What to Build\012\012### sim-core (`rust/crates/sim-core/src/components/agent_state.rs`)\012- Add `TargetKind::GatherFood` (a new variant after `Agent(AgentId)`; non-payload, like\012  Food/Water/Sleep/ConstructionSite). Document it as the carry-pickup target distinct from\012  the eat target `Food`.\012- Update `AgentState::suppresses_movement`:\012  - `Seeking { target: GatherFood }` → **true** (all `Seeking` suppress — unchanged rule, the\012    existing `matches!(self, AgentState::Seeking { .. })` already covers it; verify it does).\012  - `Consuming { target: GatherFood }` → **false** (like Food/Water/Sleep/ConstructionSite; the\012    decision system owns its own Consuming-tick exit, so no Brownian suppression).\012  - Extend the unit tests in that file to assert both.\012- No new `AgentState` variant. No `MemoryArm` change.\012\012### sim-systems (`rust/crates/sim-systems/src/runtime/decision/agent_decision.rs`)\012- `enum CascadeArm`: add `Gather` between `Combat` and `Settlement`.\012- `arm_priority_index`: `Hunger=0, Thirst=1, Fatigue=2, Construction=3, Social=4, Combat=5,\012  Gather=6, Settlement=7`.\012- `cascade_arm_memory_tag(CascadeArm::Gather)` → `None`.\012- New decision constants (place beside the existing `HUNGER_THRESHOLD` / consume-amount module\012  constants — match their current location and typing):\012  - `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS: u32` — Chebyshev radius around the agent within which a ground-food\012    tile is eligible for gathering. Default `8` (tune via harness; document the rationale).\012- Gather arm logic in the Idle cascade (see Section 3): trigger condition, target selection,\012  Seeking→Consuming→Idle transitions, pickup math.\012\012### Agent spawn sites — attach `Inventory`\012- Every site that spawns a *full runtime agent* must include `Inventory::default()` in its\012  component tuple. In Step 0 enumerate the real spawn sites and add it to each:\012  - bootstrap world seeding (the full-agent assembly in `sim-engine` — note\012    `sim-engine/src/lib.rs:523 spawn_agent` is only a `(Position, Agent)` convenience wrapper;\012    find where Hunger/Thirst/AgentState/etc. are attached and add `Inventory` there).\012  - birth: `sim-systems/.../settlement_system.rs:~695 world.spawn((...))`.\012  - EXCLUDE test-only spawns (`agent/movement.rs`, `influence/agent_sample.rs` fixtures).\012\012### Out of scope (do NOT build)\012- Storing `Inventory` → `Settlement::stockpile` (slice 2-3).\012- Eating from one's own `Inventory`/stockpile when hungry (slice 2-4). In 2-2, a hungry\012  gatherer still eats from ground tiles, not its carried food — a known, accepted MVP limit.\012- Gathering Water/Wood/Stone — **Food only** this slice (Water needs a container; Wood/Stone\012  tie into construction). `ResourceKind` keeps all four; the *behaviour* is Food-only here.\012- EventBus `ResourceGathered` event, SimBridge inventory getter, GDScript inventory display,\012  carry/depletion visualisation — all slice 2-5.\012- `MemoryArm::Gather` / memory-biased gathering.\012\012No EventBus / SimBridge / config-GDScript-mirror / localization changes in 2-2.\012\012---\012\012## Section 3: How to Implement\012\012`AgentDecisionSystem` (priority 125, every tick). Idle agents evaluate cascade arms in\012priority order; the first satisfied arm sets the `Seeking` target. Gather is evaluated only\012after Hunger/Thirst/Fatigue/Construction/Social/Combat are all ruled out, and before Settlement.\012\012**Trigger (Idle → Seeking{GatherFood}).** Fire the Gather arm when ALL hold:\0121. The agent is `Idle` and no higher-priority arm fired this tick (i.e. survival + social +\012   combat are not active — Hunger ≤ its threshold, Thirst ≤ its threshold, Fatigue ≤ its\012   threshold, no construction/social/combat target).\0122. `inventory.total() < INVENTORY_CAPACITY` (room to carry; `INVENTORY_CAPACITY = 10`).\0123. There exists a ground food tile (`SimResources::food_tiles`, count > 0) within\012   `GATHER_SCAN_RADIUS` Chebyshev of the agent's `Position`.\012If so → `AgentState::Seeking { target: TargetKind::GatherFood }` with the chosen tile as the\012`SeekTarget`. Selection = nearest eligible tile by the existing `nearest_resource_tile`\012tie-break `(distance, x, y)` (deterministic).
